<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">TRAINER GALLERY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">The set description for <em>Silver Tempest </em>mentions that it contains “a special Trainer Gallery subset of 30 cards with artwork featuring fan-favorite Trainers and Pokémon.” To see cards that may be included in future Trainer Galleries, see the <a href="/visual/tg">Trainer Galleries article</a>.</p>
</div>
